- **customer-escalation**: Packages a customer escalation for engineering, product, or leadership with full context — combines customer impact, technical detail, history, and a recommended action into a complete escalation package. Make sure to use this skill whenever the user says escalate this to engineering, package this for product, this needs to go to leadership, build an escalation for [issue], the customer is threatening to churn, this bug needs eng attention, multiple customers report the same issue, or describes a customer issue that has outgrown normal support and needs cross-functional attention. - **customer-escalation-decision-deck**: Turn buyer-pasted customer-escalation facts into an evidence-linked decision-deck outline without inventing incident causes, commitments, or account facts. ([Daisuke134/anicca](https://github.com/Daisuke134/anicca/tree/HEAD/skills/capafy/catalog/customer-escalation-decision-deck) · ★8)
- **customer-escalation-packager**: 当一个支持问题超出常规客服范畴、需上报研发/产品/安全/管理层时使用（确诊 bug 需改代码、多客户同问题、高价值客户欲流失、超 SLA 未解、疑似安全事件）；做的事是判定是否该升级、跨工单/CRM/聊天/项目板汇集上下文、量化业务影响（广度/深度/时长/营收/时压）、选对升级目标层级、为 bug 写可复现步骤，并产出结构化「升级简报」与跟进节奏；不适用于已有文档解法或可在客服层自助解决的工单、也不替你执行修复或发客户邮件（仅打包升级）。触发词：升级、escalate、escalation、上报研发、SLA breach、客户要流失、churn risk、多客户同 bug、安全升级、升级简报、复现步骤、reproduction steps ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/05-business/customer-escalation-packager) · ★11)
- **customer-escalation-pitfalls**: Use when a customer problem has moved above the usual contact, on their side or yours, and has to be handled: naming the kind of escalation (failure, relationship, commercial), the first response before the cause is known, finding out what the customer actually wants, one owner and one channel, when to bring an executive and how to brief them, the repair step after the incident closes, and the internal ask (a decision, work, presence or cover) written as one sentence with a name and a date. ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/tree/HEAD/skills/customer-escalation-pitfalls))
- **customer-escalation-recovery**: Builds an executive customer escalation recovery brief with timeline, commitments, stakeholders, risk exposure, root-cause hypotheses, response plan, and approval-required concessions. Use for major customer escalations, renewal threats, executive complaints, or service failures. ([stephenrogan/leadership-skills](https://github.com/stephenrogan/leadership-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/customer-escalation-recovery) · ★1)
- **customer-escalation-timeline**: Reconstruct customer support escalation timelines from exported tickets, event logs, handoff notes, and account context so CS, support, engineering, and CX leaders can produce a structured handoff packet, SLA/ownership risk report, and closure plan without connecting to Zendesk, Intercom, Slack, Jira, or a CRM. ([oceanfsdfsvfdsvs/practical-agent-skills](https://github.com/oceanfsdfsvfdsvs/practical-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/customer-escalation-timeline) · 2 copies)
- **customer-evidence**: Pulls customer evidence from Calven for a topic, persona, segment or competitor: verbatim quotes, recurring themes, win reasons and proof points, each with its source and date. Use when the user needs customer quotes, voice of customer, proof points or testimonials, evidence for a case study, slide, launch or reference request, or asks what customers say about a pain or feature. Do not use for a win/loss readout over a period (use win-loss-review) or a competitor battlecard (use battlecard). ([calven-ai/calven-mcp](https://github.com/calven-ai/calven-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugins/calven/skills/customer-evidence) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **customer-evidence**: Plan, run, or synthesize customer research: interviews, surveys, call and support transcripts, reviews, public communities, jobs-to-be-done, segments, a customer-language library, and evidence-grounded personas. Use when a positioning, message, conversion, pricing, or retention decision needs what customers actually say and do; use truth-file to record the settled result. ([MTEnt/special-skills](https://github.com/MTEnt/special-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/marketing-hub/skills/customer-evidence) · ★3 · 3 copies)
